gN,gL,gZ,gQ,gq,gR,gz,gA,gB,gX,ga,gM,gC,gV,gD,gW=mb,mn,mS,mU,md,mg,mx,mJ,mu,mK,mj,mf,mo,mG,gH,gt for gv=1,10,1 do gN=((gN+gq))%mN gC=mQ(mZ(gC,gN),16)gB=((gB+gC))%mN gq=mQ(mZ(gq,gB),12)gN=((gN+gq))%mN gC=mQ(mZ(gC,gN),8)gB=((gB+gC))%mN gq=mQ(mZ(gq,gB),7)gL=((gL+gR))%mN gV=mQ(mZ(gV,gL),16)gX=((gX+gV))%mN gR=mQ(mZ(gR,gX),12)gL=((gL+gR))%mN gV=mQ(mZ(gV,gL),8)gX=((gX+gV))%mN gR=mQ(mZ(gR,gX),7)gZ=((gZ+gz))%mN gD=mQ(mZ(gD,gZ),16)ga=((ga+gD))%mN gz=mQ(mZ(gz,ga),12)gZ=((gZ+gz))%mN gD=mQ(mZ(gD,gZ),8)ga=((ga+gD))%mN gz=mQ(mZ(gz,ga),7)gQ=((gQ+gA))%mN gW=mQ(mZ(gW,gQ),16)gM=((gM+gW))%mN gA=mQ(mZ(gA,gM),12)gQ=((gQ+gA))%mN gW=mQ(mZ(gW,gQ),8)gM=((gM+gW))%mN gA=mQ(mZ(gA,gM),7)gN=((gN+gR))%mN gW=mQ(mZ(gW,gN),16)ga=((ga+gW))%mN gR=mQ(mZ(gR,ga),12)gN=((gN+gR))%mN gW=mQ(mZ(gW,gN),8)ga=((ga+gW))%mN gR=mQ(mZ(gR,ga),7)gL=((gL+gz))%mN gC=mQ(mZ(gC,gL),16)gM=((gM+gC))%mN gz=mQ(mZ(gz,gM),12)gL=((gL+gz))%mN gC=mQ(mZ(gC,gL),8)gM=((gM+gC))%mN gz=mQ(mZ(gz,gM),7)gZ=((gZ+gA))%mN gV=mQ(mZ(gV,gZ),16)gB=((gB+gV))%mN gA=mQ(mZ(gA,gB),12)gZ=((gZ+gA))%mN gV=mQ(mZ(gV,gZ),8)gB=((gB+gV))%mN gA=mQ(mZ(gA,gB),7)gQ=((gQ+gq))%mN gD=mQ(mZ(gD,gQ),16)gX=((gX+gD))%mN gq=mQ(mZ(gq,gX),12)gQ=((gQ+gq))%mN gD=mQ(mZ(gD,gQ),8)gX=((gX+gD))%mN gq=mQ(mZ(gq,gX),7)end return{((gN+mb))%mN,((gL+mn))%mN,((gZ+mS))%mN;((gQ+mU))%mN,((gq+md))%mN,((gR+mg))%mN;((gz+mx))%mN,((gA+mJ))%mN;((gB+mu))%mN,((gX+mK))%mN;((ga+mj))%mN;((gM+mf))%mN;((gC+mo))%mN,((gV+mG))%mN;((gD+gH))%mN;((gW+gt))%mN}end local function gk(gy,gE,gF,gw,gP,gp)if#gy==0 then return gy end gF,gw,gP,gp=gF or 1,gw or 0,gP or 0,gp or 0 local gT,gc={},1 while gc<=#gy do local gY=mY(gE,gF,gw,gP,gp)for gI=1,16,1 do local gr=gY[gI]for gh=1,4,1 do if gc>#gy then break end gT[gc]=dG(mZ(df(gy,gc),gr%256))gr,gc=dj(gr/256),gc+1 end end gF=((gF+1))%mN end return mH(gT)end local function ge(gs,gb,gn)return mY(gs,0,gb,gn%mN,dj(gn/mN))end local function gS(gU,gd,gm,gg,gx)local gJ=ge(gx,gm,gg)local gu={mZ(gd,gJ[1])}for gK=1,8,1 do gu[gK+1]=mZ(gU[gK],gJ[gK+1])end gu[10]=gJ[10]return gu end local function gj(gf,go,gG,xH)local xt=ge(xH,go,gG)return{mZ(gf,xt[1]);xt[10]}end local function xN(xL,xZ,xQ,xq,xR)local xz=ge(xR,xQ,xq)return xL[2]==xz[10]and xL[1]==mZ(xZ,xz[1])end local function xA(xB,xX,xa,xM,xC)local xV=ge(xC,xa,xM)if xB[10]~=xV[10]or xB[1]~=mZ(xX,xV[1])then return nil end local xD={}for xW=1,8,1 do xD[xW]=mZ(xB[xW+1],xV[xW+1])end return xD end local function xv(xk)local xy={}for xE=1,#xk,1 do local xF=xk[xE]xy[xE]=dG(xF%256,dj(xF/256)%256,dj(xF/65536)%256,dj(xF/16777216)%256)end return mH(xy)end return{block=mY;crypt=gk,seal=gS;sealAdmission=gj;admit=xN,resolve=xA,packWords=xv}end))()local xP={2430438661,2687090336,4041719685,1370325801;334530300,2747034903,2908788870;482819142}local function xp(xT,xc,xY)local xI=(HN(903466230))local xr={}local xh=HL[HN(667507153)][HN(915872320)]for xe=1,#xI,1 do xr[xh(xI,xe)]=xe-1 end local xs=HL[HN(667507153)][HN(1300125103)]local xw[HN(1454870834)](xm,xc,xY)end if HL[HN(288192956)](xT)~=(HN(667507153))then return xT end local xg=xs(xT)local xx={}local xJ=1 while xJ<=xg do local xu=(xg-xJ)+1 local xj=xu>=5 and 5 or xu local xf=0 local xo=xj>1 for xG=0,4,1 do local JH if xG<xj then JH=xr[xh(xT,xJ+xG)]if not JH then xo=false break end else JH=84 end xf=xf*85+JH end if xo then local Jt=xb(xf/16777216)%256 local JN=xb(xf/65536)%256 local JL=xb(xf/256)%256 local JZ=xf%256 if xj==5 then xS(xx,xn(Jt,JN,JL,JZ))elseif xj==4 then xS(xx,xn(Jt,JN,JL))elseif xj==3 then xS(xx,xn(Jt,JN))elseif xj==2 then xS(xx,xn(Jt))end end xJ=xJ+xj end return xd(xU(xx))end local JQ={}local Jh=((function(Jq,JR,Jz,JA)local JB=xp(Jz,JA)local JX,Ja=HL[HN(667507153)][HN(915872320)],HL[HN(667507153)][HN(1466088800)]local function JM(JC,JV)local JD,JW,Jv,Jk=JX(JC,JV,JV+3)return((JD+JW*256)+Jv*65536)+Jk*16777216 end local Jy={}local JE,JF=1,1 for Jw=1,#JB,4 do local JO=JM(JB,Jw)local JP=JO%65536 local Jp=HL[HN(617146693)][HN(1082975238)](JO/65536)Jy[JP]={JE;Jp;JF}JE,JF=JE+Jp,JF+1 end JB=nil return function(JT)local Jc=Jy[JT]if not Jc then return nil end local
